3919 — landmine rear lunge

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3919 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A reverse lunge holding a landmine bar at the chest, working the quads and glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold the end of a landmine bar at your chest. Stand tall with feet hip-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3919.json#/instructions/0, 3919.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 3919.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3919.json#/instructions/1

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Straight Olympic barbell, Landmine swivel anchor. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3919.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Hold the end of a landmine bar at your chest. 3919.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand tall with feet hip-width apart. 3919.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step one foot back and lower into a lunge. 3919.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your front knee over your ankle. 3919.json#/instructions/3
  5. Push through the front foot to stand. 3919.json#/instructions/4
  6. Alternate legs. 3919.json#/instructions/5

3920 — cable triceps pushdown on floor

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3920 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A triceps pushdown performed while sitting or lying on the floor with the cable set low.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set a cable at a low or mid position and sit on the floor facing it. Hold the handle with elbows tucked at your sides.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3920.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set a cable at a low or mid position and sit on the floor facing it. 3920.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold the handle with elbows tucked at your sides. 3920.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press the handle down until your arms are straight. 3920.json#/instructions/2

  5. Return slowly to the start. 3920.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keep your upper arms still. 3920.json#/instructions/5
